# plan-85 per-`file:line` emission-site work-list (generated snapshot)

Generated on base `c0c30e70a` by the grep commands recorded in `plan-85-census.md`
(§"Per-file:line work-list generation"). This is a **reproducible snapshot**, not a
hand-curated list — regenerate it with those commands at any time (it goes stale as
plan-85-B/C convert files, which is expected; the census per-file summary is the
durable ledger).

Each site's **default** target token follows the deterministic rule in the census:

- `abi::RET[k]` / `return_register()` → `%retMFB[k]` (a genuine MFB result), or `%retC[k]`
  where the value is returned **straight from a C call** (rax-exact) — resolved per site
  in plan-85-B.
- `RESULT_{TAG,VALUE,ERROR_MESSAGE,ERROR_SOURCE}_REGISTER` → `%retMFB[0..3]` (plan-85-C).
- `abi::SYSARG[k]` → `%argSys[k]`; a syscall result → `%retSys`.
- `abi::ARG[k]` / `argument_register(k)` → `%argC[k]` when the arg feeds a `_mfb_*` /
  arena / `emit_symbol_call` / syscall C boundary, else `%argMFB[k]` (an MFB-function
  call). The C-vs-MFB split is the one distinction that needs the **callee** at the site;
  plan-85-B reads it from the `MFB_BUG387_AUDIT=1` `@src` sweep as each file converts.

The raw per-`file:line` sites follow, split by category. The concatenated raw form (no
section headers) is also in `plan-85-worklist-raw.txt`.

## B — single-role ARG sites (default %%argC to a _mfb_*/arena/syscall callee, else %%argMFB)
